## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route: overview, Parkland, financial profile, returns, segments, then growth, with section dividers (3, 12, 27) and a consistent template of navy bars, orange titles and a footer. Most content pages state their point in the title, and the charts are readable, for example the dual-axis chart on slide 14 with its COVID callout and the DCF growth chart on slide 10. Weaknesses are consequential: slide 2 is a wall of tiny legal text, many pages are dense with small bullets and microscopic footnotes (4, 8, 13, 17, 21), and the segment overview slides (13, 17, 21, 25) repeat a faint template with low-contrast grey panels and pale icons. The ending is weak, since slide 33 on midstream multiples is followed only by a logo page with no summary or call to action, and the deck mostly lists claims rather than building to a conclusion.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent navy and orange system, the claim-style titles, the cover photo collage (slide 1), and the clear annotated charts on slides 10, 11 and 14.

**Most useful repair:** Shrink or move the legal text on slide 2 to an appendix, enlarge or cut the footnotes, and strengthen contrast on the grey overview panels and faded icons on slides 13, 17, 21 and 25.

**What would change the score:** I would trim the bullet-heavy pages to one message each, add a closing summary slide, and fix slide 28, where the timeline boxes run to the slide edge and wrap awkwardly.

_Confidence high. 34 of 34 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a standard forward-looking statements disclaimer and non-GAAP definitions, followed by investor relations contacts. It then moves into an investment overview covering the Parkland acquisition, the company's scale across fuel distribution and energy infrastructure, its balance sheet and distribution policy, and a structure chart for the Sunoco LP and SunocoCorp LLC entities. Next comes a section on distribution per unit growth and total return compared with the S&P 500 and midstream peers. The business segments section follows, covering fuel distribution, terminals, pipeline systems and the integrated refinery and marketing system, each with market context and performance charts. The deck then turns to future growth, including a timeline of acquisitions since 2018, a bolt-on strategy for fuel distribution, geographic growth priorities and a closing slide on midstream acquisition multiples. Its visual approach relies on bold section dividers, dense bullet text, and charts that pair company metrics with industry benchmarks.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides or pages does the Sunoco LP investor presentation have?**

The Sunoco LP investor presentation has 34 pages. Slide 2 contains the forward-looking statements disclaimer and non-GAAP definitions, and slide 4 summarizes fuel distribution operations and terminals.

**What does the Sunoco LP investor presentation say about its fuel distribution scale?**

Slide 4 states that Sunoco LP distributed more than 15 billion gallons, operates about 11,000 contracted locations, and serves 32 countries and territories. Slide 13 adds that the company operates about 200 company-operated locations concentrated in high-margin markets.

**What does the Sunoco LP investor presentation say about its distribution growth?**

Slide 8 lists annual distribution increases of 2% in 2023 and 4% in 2024, and says the company is targeting ongoing annual growth of at least 5%. The same slide gives an attractive distribution yield of about 7% as of 10/31/25.

**What financial benefits does Sunoco LP expect from the Parkland acquisition according to the deck?**

Slide 5 says the Parkland acquisition is expected to deliver 10%+ accretion to DCF per common unit by year three and to capture at least $250 million in run-rate synergies by year three. It also states the company expects to return to about 4x leverage within the first year.

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent navy and orange system, the claim-style titles, the cover photo collage (slide 1), and the clear annotated charts on slides 10, 11 and 14.

**Text**

SUNOCO LP / Investor Presentation / November 2025

**Layout**

Full-bleed composition in two parts. The top band is a dark navy header, about 32% of the slide height, holding the logo, title and date left-aligned at about 8.8% from the left edge. Below it, six photographs fill the full width in two rows of three, with diagonal cut edges between neighbouring photos. There is no footer, slide number or other furniture.

**Visuals**

Six photographs arranged as a 3 by 2 mosaic under the navy band, with slanted boundaries between tiles. Top row, left to right: (1) a close-up rear view of a dark blue tanker trailer with a large yellow '94 OCTANE' logo and a small yellow SUNOCO diamond, a PT-432V5 licence plate, red and white bumpers and 'CAUTION WIDE TURNS' signs, under a bright blue sky with white clouds. (2) An aerial night view of about eight white storage tanks with black roofs and lit rims, each with a SUNOCO LP logo, on a dark gravel site with a small grey building. (3) A dusk view with a pink and orange sky, showing a blue-and-yellow SUNOCO canopy fascia with a large yellow chevron and a SUNOCO wordmark over a lit fuel station ceiling. Bottom row, left to right: (4) a low-angle view up through white steel pipe-like columns against a clear blue sky, with gravel below. (5) A dusk view of a fuel canopy with a navy fascia and '94 OCTANE' signage, brick pillars, lit pumps, a red sports car and a dark blue Corvette parked in front, under a purple and orange sunset. (6) An aerial view of large white storage tanks beside turquoise sea and a sandy beach with a headland, on a concrete site. Lighting is natural in the daytime photos and artificial in the night and dusk photos. The photos are cropped to fill their tiles, with no borders and no overlay. Dominant colours are blue, white, pink-orange and navy. A small orange-red vertical accent bar sits at the left of the title block, outside the photographs.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF slide background, with the navy #262F47 header band covering the top third and the photo mosaic covering the lower two-thirds. Navy header band with a white logo and white title text. Title is a bold sans-serif (Arial-like, bold) at about 4.6% of slide height, subtitle a regular sans-serif (Arial-like) at about 3.7%. The orange-red accent bar is about #E8502F. The photo mosaic is full-bleed with hard edges and no rounded corners, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 3

**Text**

SUNOCO LP
Investment Overview

**Layout**

Single centred title on a full-bleed photographic section divider. The title sits at the vertical middle of the slide, centred horizontally, with the logo in the top-left corner on a white tab. The photo is inset from the slide edges, leaving a thin white margin on all four sides. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Photograph: aerial view of a tank farm of large cylindrical storage tanks with grey and white domed roofs, pipework and small structures visible between them. The tanks fill the frame from edge to edge, shot from above at an oblique angle. The photo is cropped by the inset box and tinted by a navy translucent overlay. The lower quarter carries a large, faint, slanted letterform watermark in a lighter slate tone. Logo: SUNOCO LP wordmark in bold italic navy capitals with a small registered-trademark mark, set on a white tab in the top-left corner.

**Design**

Background is white #FFFFFF framing a navy-tinted photograph. Title is white bold sans-serif (Arial/Helvetica Bold) with a height of about 6.5% of the slide height. The logo is navy #2B3A5C. The photo has a navy overlay of about #2A3550 at roughly 70% opacity. The watermark is a lighter slate #4A5570 at low opacity. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Full-width two-line bold title centred across the top (about 3% margin from the top edge). A large map fills the left and centre (about 6% to 80% of the width, 14% to 94% of the height), with a stacked column of five key figures on the right (about 84% to 95% of the width). A Hawaii inset sits bottom-left and a Europe inset sits top-right of the map. A thin orange rule runs above the footnotes with the Sunoco LP logo at bottom-left and the page number 4 at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

A map of North America and the Caribbean, with a Europe inset and a Hawaii inset. Contiguous US and southern Canada are filled dark navy (#262F47) as fuel distribution operations. Mexico, Canada's far north, Central America and South America are light grey (about #E3E3E3). Over the navy areas are small blue (#3D8BF5 approximately) dots marking contracted fuel locations, dense in the eastern US and sparse in the west. Red (#FF0000) circular dots mark terminals across the US, Canada, Caribbean, and Europe. Green (#4CD628 approximately) dots mark transmix/race fuel sites, including points in Quebec, Ohio, Pennsylvania, Virginia, Texas, and Alabama. A magenta (#E020E0 approximately) triangle marks the Burnaby Refinery on the British Columbia coast. Yellow (#F5E21A approximately) lines mark pipelines, concentrated in Texas, Oklahoma, Kansas, the Gulf coast, and the Midwest. The Europe inset is a rounded-corner box showing Ireland, the UK, Benelux, Germany, Poland and neighbouring countries in mid grey with a light grey surround, with red terminal dots in Belgium, the Netherlands, Germany, Poland and Ireland. The Hawaii inset is a rounded-corner box with navy islands, blue contracted dots and red terminal dots. A legend with navy square and coloured dot or line keys sits to the right of the map. The map is not cropped at the edges except at the bottom right where it stops at the frame edge. No photographs or logos other than the Sunoco LP logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) in orange-red #FA4931, cap height about 4.8% of slide height, centred. Key figures in bold sans-serif #FA4931 at about 3.3% of slide height; their labels in regular sans-serif #262F47 at about 2.3%. Footnotes in italic sans-serif #7F7F7F at about 1.1%. Page number in regular sans-serif #262F47. Orange-red rule #FA4931 about 0.3% of height thick. Map uses navy #262F47 fill with thin white state borders. Insets have rounded corners about 1% radius with thin dark grey outlines. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Places the Parkland acquisition benefits in three labeled columns with bolded headline claims, making the deal's financial case scannable at a glance.

**Text**

Parkland Acquisition Has
Strengthened SUN’s Foundation
Stable Cash Flow
Creates largest independent(1) fuel distributor in the Americas
Delivers vast scale for optionality and cost advantages
Diversifies portfolio and optimizes for stability with upside
Adds midstream assets in the Greater Caribbean, Canada, and the U.S.
Compelling Financial Benefits
Delivers 10%+ accretion to DCF(2) per common unit by year three … Immediately accretive in year one
Captures at least $250 million in run-rate synergies by year three
Returns to ~4x leverage target within the first year
Exceeds original transaction financing assumptions … better interest rates will result in lower financing costs by >$40 million/year
Greater Financial Flexibility
Expects to generate a >50% increase in free cash flow(3) vs. SUN stand-alone
Strengthens ability to continue to execute on capital allocation strategy
Secure and growing distribution
Disciplined investment in growth opportunities

**Layout**

Single-column layout on a white field with three stacked full-width sections, each a navy header bar followed by red-bulleted text lines, with the centered two-line red title at the top. The SUNOCO LP logo sits bottom-left, a thin red rule runs across the footer to the right of it, the slide number sits bottom-right, and three small italic footnotes sit below the rule starting at the logo's text column; left margin is about 4 percent of slide width and the header bars span about 4 to 96 percent.

**Visuals**

none. The only non-text graphic is the SUNOCO LP wordmark logo in the footer (navy wordmark with italic 'LP', 288 by 39 pixels) and a thin red horizontal rule, which are described under elements.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or imagery. Title text is red-orange #FA4931 in a bold sans-serif (Arial/Helvetica Bold), about 5.2 percent of slide height per line, centered. Section headers are white bold sans-serif (Arial Bold, about 2.9 percent of slide height) on flat navy #262F47 rectangular bars with square corners and no shadow. Body bullets are regular-weight Arial in navy #262F47 at about 2.3 percent of slide height, with red-orange #FB4830 round bullet dots; second-level bullets use #FA4931. Footnotes are italic Arial in grey #7F7F7F at about 1.1 percent of slide height. Page number is regular Arial in navy #262F47. Accent rule is red-orange #FA4931.

### Slide 6

**Text**

Compelling Long-Term Investment
Stable Cash Flow
Strong Financial Profile
Accretive Growth
Diversified Portfolio – Operations across the U.S., Canada, the Greater Caribbean, and Europe
Fuel Distribution – Proven history of generating stable income in various market environments; scale and SUN’s proprietary brands are key differentiators that enable higher margin capture
Midstream – Critical nature of assets ensures long-term operations; vertical integration supports high asset utilization and more margin capture along the value chain
Strong Balance Sheet and Liquidity Position – Provides financial flexibility for growth and reduces risk
Growing Income for Investors – Consistently maintained or increased distributions since IPO in 2012
Balanced and Disciplined Capital Allocation Strategy – Focused on distribution growth, accretive growth opportunities, and a strong balance sheet
Consistent Value Creation for Unitholders – Only AMZI constituent to grow DCF(1) per common unit for the last eight consecutive years (2017 - 2024); expect continued growth in 2025 and beyond
Proven Capital Allocation – Since 2017, deployed $19 billion of growth and acquisition capital, increasing DCF(1) per common unit by approximately 60%
Significant Growth Opportunities – Expansive, diversified, and accretive pipeline of investment opportunities
(1) Distributable Cash Flow, as adjusted is a non-GAAP measure. For a definition, please see slide 2

A thin orange-red rule runs across the lower area at about 94.6% height, with the Sunoco LP logo at bottom left, the footnote under the rule's left portion, and the page number 6 at bottom right.

none

**Layout**

A single column chart occupies the centre of the slide with the title block centred at the top, a red footer rule with the SUNOCO LP logo at bottom left, the footnote below it and the slide number at bottom right. Callout labels sit in a right-hand column aligned to the stacked brackets of the last bar, and the chart axis baseline runs from about x 5 to x 82.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart with five columns: 2022, 2023, 2024, SUN TTM 6/30/25, and Combined pro forma TTM 6/30/25. Series are Fuel Distribution (navy, top segment), Midstream (red, middle segment) and Refinery (grey, bottom segment, present only on the combined column). No y-axis, values, units or gridlines are printed; heights are unlabelled, so relative heights are about: 2022 total about 59 percent of plot height with Midstream a thin band of about 4 percent; 2023 total about 60 percent with Midstream about 4 percent; 2024 total about 75 percent with Midstream about 35 percent; SUN total about 83 percent with Midstream about 52 percent; Combined total about 100 percent with Midstream about 35 percent of height and Refinery about 9 percent. Legend below the chart in one row: navy square Fuel Distribution, red square Midstream, grey square Refinery. Two red curly brackets on the right of the combined column: the upper bracket spans the navy Fuel Distribution segment and is labelled by a dashed red-outlined callout; the lower bracket spans the red Midstream segment and is labelled by a second dashed red-outlined callout. Footnote marker (1) is superscripted in the first callout. Logo: SUNOCO LP wordmark in navy bold italic sans-serif. A thin red horizontal rule runs from about x 16.5 to x 92 at the footer level.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title in bold red-orange sans-serif (Arial Bold style), centred, two lines, about 4.9 percent of slide height per line. Callout and legend text in bold navy sans-serif (Arial Bold for callouts, Arial Regular for legend and axis labels) at about 2.0 percent of slide height. Axis labels in regular navy sans-serif at about 1.6 percent. Footnote in italic grey sans-serif at about 1.1 percent. Colours: title red-orange #FA4931; callout and axis text navy #262F47; Fuel Distribution navy #262F47; Midstream red-orange #FA4931; Refinery light grey #B8BBC2; footnote grey #7F7F7F. Callout boxes have dashed red-orange #FA4931 borders about 1 pt wide with square corners and no shadow; brackets are solid red-orange #FA4931 strokes about 2 pt. Baseline is a thin light grey line #D9D9D9. Footer rule is a solid red-orange #FA4931 line about 0.3 percent of slide height thick.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Flexibility and Optionality for Investors
Sunoco LP (NYSE: SUN)
SunocoCorp LLC (NYSE: SUNC)
• Structured as a partnership
• Pass-through entity; not subject to corporate taxation
• K-1 issuer
• Owns 100% of assets and liabilities
• Structured as an LLC
• Taxed as a corporation
• 1099 issuer
• Sole assets are Sunoco LP units; no debt
• Minimal corporate income taxes expected for at least five years
Operating Assets and Subsidiaries
Sunoco LP NYSE:SUN
SUN Distributions
SUN Unitholders ~73% Limited Partner Interest in SUN
SunocoCorp LLC NYSE:SUNC ~27% Limited Partner Interest in SUN
SUNC Dividends
SUNC Shareholders
SUNOCO LP
9

**Layout**

Two-column upper section with two equal dark navy header bars side by side, followed by a central vertical flow diagram on the left with two ellipses on the right; a full-width title is centred at the top, a thin orange rule runs along the bottom with the logo at bottom left and the page number at bottom right. Left margin about 3.9% and right margin about 3.9%; the title is centred horizontally, all header and bullet text is left-aligned.

**Visuals**

Diagram with a vertical flow of three orange-outlined rectangles on the left, connected by solid dark navy arrows, and two navy-outlined ellipses on the right connected by dashed navy arrows. Top rectangle 'Operating Assets and Subsidiaries' contains four small dark navy line icons in a row: a cement mixer truck, a storage tank, a pipe valve with fittings, and a refinery/plant tower cluster. Solid downward arrow from this rectangle to the middle rectangle 'Sunoco LP NYSE:SUN'. Solid downward arrow labelled 'SUN Distributions' from the middle rectangle to the bottom rectangle 'SunocoCorp LLC NYSE:SUNC ~27% Limited Partner Interest in SUN'. Dashed arrow labelled 'SUN Distributions' runs right from the middle rectangle to the ellipse 'SUN Unitholders ~73% Limited Partner Interest in SUN'. Dashed arrow labelled 'SUNC Dividends' runs right from the bottom rectangle to the ellipse 'SUNC Shareholders'. No photographs; no charts; no printed values beyond the stated percentages.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title bold sans-serif (Arial/Helvetica style) in orange-red #FA4931, large (about 4.9% of slide height). Header labels bold white sans-serif on navy bars #262F47, about 2.9% of slide height. Body bullets regular sans-serif in navy #262F47, about 2.3% of slide height; bullet glyphs orange-red #FA4931. Diagram labels bold navy #262F47 about 2.6%; small annotations regular navy about 2.0%. Orange-red #FA4931 accents for the rules and rectangle outlines; navy #262F47 for ellipse outlines, arrows and icons. Rectangle outlines about 0.5% of slide height thick, square corners; ellipse outlines about 0.4% thick. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Review: Most content pages state their point in the title, and the charts are readable, for example the dual-axis chart on slide 14 with its COVID callout and the DCF growth chart on slide 10.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent navy and orange system, the claim-style titles, the cover photo collage (slide 1), and the clear annotated charts on slides 10, 11 and 14.

**Text**

Meaningful and Consistent Growth / Since 2017, SUN Has Materially Grown DCF(1) / per Common Unit by ~60%… / …Expanding Capital / Allocation Opportunities / $6.00 / $5.50 / $5.00 / $4.50 / $4.00 / $3.50 / $3.00 / $2.50 / 2017 2018 2019 2020 2021 2022 2023 2024 TTM 2Q25 / DCF(1) per Common Unit / Distribution per Common Unit / Continuation of proven capital / allocation strategy: / • Secure and growing distribution / • Disciplined investment in / growth opportunities / • Strong balance sheet / ~7% CAGR (2017 – TTM 2Q25) / (1) Distributable Cash Flow, as adjusted is a non-GAAP measure. For a definition, please see slide 2 / 10

**Layout**

Single-slide content area with a centred bold title at the top, two dark navy header bands side by side (a wide left band over a bar chart and a narrow right band over a bullet text block), a bottom footer row holding the SUNOCO LP logo at left, a thin red rule running to a page number at right, and the footnote beneath the logo line; the left chart occupies about the left 60 percent and the right text block about the right 25 percent, with left margin about 5.5 percent and right margin about 4 percent.

**Visuals**

Combined column and line chart. Title of chart: none printed; header band above it reads Since 2017, SUN Has Materially Grown DCF(1) per Common Unit by ~60%. Y-axis: Distributable cash flow and distribution in US dollars per common unit, linear, range $2.50 to $6.00, gridlines absent, labels at $0.50 steps. X-axis: fiscal years 2017, 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, and TTM 2Q25 (trailing twelve months to second quarter 2025). Series 1, DCF per Common Unit, navy columns with values about 3.50 (2017), about 3.88 (2018), about 3.89 (2019), about 4.28 (2020), about 4.48 (2021), about 5.10 (2022), about 5.17 (2023), about 5.60 (2024), about 5.67 (TTM 2Q25); no values are printed so all are estimates. Series 2, Distribution per Common Unit, green line stepping with values about 3.34 for 2017 through 2022, about 3.50 for 2023, about 3.70 for 2024, and about 3.87 for TTM 2Q25; estimates. Legend below the x-axis: navy square labelled DCF(1) per Common Unit and green line labelled Distribution per Common Unit. Annotation: a red block arrow with thin navy outline rising from the 2017 column to above the 2024 column, with white rotated text ~7% CAGR (2017 – TTM 2Q25) lying along it. Highlighted points: none marked with symbols. Right side: a navy right-facing brace bracketing the bullet text.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate slide on white with navy and red-orange accents plus a green data line. Title is bold red-orange #FA4931 sans-serif (Arial Bold style), about 4.9 percent of slide height, centred. Header bands are solid navy #262F47 with white bold sans-serif text (Arial Bold style) about 2.9 percent of slide height, centred. Body text in the right block is regular sans-serif (Arial style) in navy #262F47 about 2.3 percent of slide height, with red-orange #FA4931 bullet dots. Axis labels are regular navy sans-serif about 1.6 percent. Footnote is italic grey #7F7F7F sans-serif about 1.1 percent. Page number regular navy about 1.6 percent. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients; the arrow has a thin navy outline; the brace is a thin navy line.

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent navy and orange system, the claim-style titles, the cover photo collage (slide 1), and the clear annotated charts on slides 10, 11 and 14.

**Text**

SUN Continues to Offer Material Upside / After Eight Years of Outperformance… / …SUN Continues to Trade at Attractive / Valuations(2) vs. the S&P 500… / Lowest Decile EV / AEBITDA / Top Decile Dividend Yield / …and Midstream Energy Peers(3) / 425% / SUN +384%(1) / (20% annual return) / 325% / 225% / S&P 500 +233%(1) / (15% annual return) / Total Return1 / 125% / 11.0x / 25% / AMZI +51%(1) / (5% annual return) / LTM EV/AEBITDA(4) / 9.0x / (75%) / Peer Average ~9.7x / 2017 2018 2019 2020 2021 2022 2023 2024 2025 / SUN / S&P 500 / AMZI / 7.0x / Peer 1 / SUN / Peer 2 / Peer 3 / Peer 4 / Peer 5 / Peer 6 / Peer 7 / Peer 8 / Peer 9 / Peer 10 / Peer 11 / SUNOCO LP / 11 / (1) Source: Bloomberg, as of 10/31/25. Defined as price appreciation plus reinvestment of dividends/distributions / (2) Source: FactSet, as of 10/31/25 / (3) Peer group includes: CQP, DKL, EPD, ET, GEL, GLP, HESM, MPLX, PAA, USAC, WES / (4) Source: FactSet EV = Current Market cap + preferred equity + minority interest + net debt; EBITDA = TTM Adjusted EBITDA, as of 2Q25

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a left column (about x 4 to 48%) holding a navy header bar and a line chart, and a right column (about x 52 to 96%) holding a navy header bar, two checkmark rows, a second navy header bar and a bar chart. The title is centred across the top at about y 3 to 8%, with empty space beneath it before the column headers at y 15.6%. The SUNOCO LP logo sits bottom-left at y 94.2%, a red rule runs horizontally at about y 94.7% from the logo to the page number, the page number 11 sits bottom-right at x 93.6%, and footnotes are set in two columns beneath the rule at y 96 to 100%.

**Visuals**

Chart 1 (left): line chart titled by the header 'After Eight Years of Outperformance…', with a y-axis titled 'Total Return1' showing labelled ticks (75%), 25%, 125%, 225%, 325%, 425% and an x-axis of years 2017 to 2025 (annual tick labels, no units printed beyond the percent axis). Three series with a legend at the bottom reading SUN (navy line), S&P 500 (green line), AMZI (red line). Endpoint labels: SUN +384% (20% annual return) in navy at the top right, S&P 500 +233% (15% annual return) in green, AMZI +51% (5% annual return) in red. SUN starts at 0% in 2017, dips to about (50%) near early 2020 and ends near 384%. S&P 500 starts at 0%, dips to about 0% in 2020, and ends at +233%. AMZI starts at 0%, falls to about (60%) in 2020 and ends at +51%. A thin grey horizontal baseline sits at 0%. No other values are printed; intermediate values are not labelled. Two checkmark icons: each is a red check mark inside a navy-outlined square box (about 118 by 117 pixels), the first beside 'Lowest Decile EV / AEBITDA' and the second beside 'Top Decile Dividend Yield'. Chart 2 (right bottom): vertical bar chart titled by the header '…and Midstream Energy Peers(3)', y-axis titled 'LTM EV/AEBITDA(4)' with labelled ticks 7.0x, 9.0x, 11.0x (axis starts at 7.0x). X-axis categories in order: Peer 1, SUN, Peer 2 through Peer 11. Bars are navy (#262F47) except the SUN bar which is red (#FA4931). Values are not printed; approximate heights: Peer 1 about 8.1x, SUN about 8.6x, Peer 2 about 8.6x, Peer 3 about 9.2x, Peer 4 about 9.3x, Peer 5 about 9.4x, Peer 6 about 9.7x, Peer 7 about 10.1x, Peer 8 about 10.3x, Peer 9 about 10.5x, Peer 10 about 10.7x, Peer 11 about 10.9x. A red dashed horizontal line marks Peer Average ~9.7x across the chart. Logo: SUNOCO LP wordmark in navy bold italic at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF). Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold or Helvetica Bold-like), red-orange #FA4931, about 4.9% of slide height, centred. Header bars are solid navy #262F47 rectangles with square corners and no shadow, with white (#FFFFFF) bold sans-serif header text about 2.9% of slide height. Body and axis text is navy #262F47, regular sans-serif about 1.5% of slide height; checkmark labels are bold navy about 2.45%. Chart series colours: SUN navy #262F47, S&P 500 green #00B050, AMZI red #FA4931. Footnotes are italic grey #7F7F7F sans-serif about 1.1% of slide height. Thin rules and chart gridlines are light grey; the baseline is light grey #D9D9D9.

### Slide 12

**Text**

SUNOCO LP
Business Segments

**Layout**

Full-bleed white slide with a navy-tinted aerial photograph inset from the edges, leaving a white margin on the left, right and bottom and a white tab at top-left holding the logo. The logo sits top-left at about 6.5% from the left and 5.5% from the top. The title is centred horizontally in the upper-middle of the photograph, at about 37% from the top. There is no footer, subtitle, body text or slide number.

**Visuals**

Aerial photograph of large cylindrical white storage tanks with floating roofs, seen from above at an oblique angle, with pipework and a valve manifold visible at the right edge and in the upper middle. The photograph is full-width within the inset frame, not cropped to a shape, and has a dark navy translucent overlay (roughly 70% opacity, colour about #2A3550) that darkens and desaturates it. Across the lower part of the photograph is a large translucent slanted lettering watermark in lighter grey-blue (about #4A5468 at about 35% opacity) forming oversized letterforms with diagonal strokes. Logo: the SUNOCO LP wordmark in bold, slanted, heavy navy capitals (about #1F2A44) with a small registered mark after LP, placed on the white tab at top-left.

**Design**

Background white (#FFFFFF) frame around a navy-tinted photograph. Title in white (#FFFFFF) bold sans-serif, Arial or Helvetica style, sized about 6.5% of slide height, with normal letter case. Logo navy (#1F2A44). Overlay navy (#2A3550). The white tab at top-left has a straight diagonal right edge running from the top of the photograph down-left to the bottom of the tab. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners or borders are visible.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

Overlay opacity about 70%. Top-left corner is covered by the white logo tab, which has a diagonal right edge from about x 34% at the top to about x 32% at its bottom edge at y 7%.)
- logo at x 6.5, y 5.5, w 22.7, h 3.9 [#1F2A44]: SUNOCO LP logo (Navy wordmark on white tab, bold slanted capitals with registered mark. Sits above the photograph at top-left.)

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Shrink or move the legal text on slide 2 to an appendix, enlarge or cut the footnotes, and strengthen contrast on the grey overview panels and faded icons on slides 13, 17, 21 and 25.

**Text**

Diverse and Stable Business Segments
Fuel Distribution Overview
• Largest independent(1) fuel distributor in the Americas with over
15 billion gallons distributed across a broad network
• Stable margins driven by scale, diversity, and proprietary brands
• Network of ~11,000 contracted locations provides diverse mix of
geography and channels
• ~200 company-operated locations strategically concentrated in
high-margin markets with durable competitive advantages
• Proprietary fuel brands enable long-term supply contracts with
expanded margins
• Segment gross margin anchored by stable, ratable lease income from
real estate portfolio and 7-Eleven take-or-pay contract
Key SUN Investment Highlights
• History of stable and consistent growth through economic shocks and
commodity cycles
• SUN well positioned within industry dynamics
• Geographic diversity creates stability with growth opportunities
13
(1) Independent refers to not linked to any refinery other than the Burnaby Refinery located in British Columbia

**Layout**

Single-title slide with a centred red title across the top and a large light-grey content panel occupying the right three-quarters of the slide, with a column of four grey line icons down the left margin. The panel holds two centred section headings, each with a full-width navy rule beneath, and bulleted text left-aligned inside. Sunoco LP logo sits bottom-left, a thin red footer rule runs across the bottom with the page number at its right end and a small italic footnote below the rule.

**Visuals**

Four line-art icons stacked down the left margin, all outline style with no fill: (1) top, navy #262F47 tanker truck with a cylindrical tank body, cab and four wheels, facing right; (2) grey #A6A6A6 pair of cylindrical storage tanks of different heights, one larger and one smaller, banded with vertical stripes; (3) grey #A6A6A6 pipeline with a valve and wheel on top and two elbow pipes, forming an inverted U; (4) grey #A6A6A6 refinery cluster of towers, columns and a vessel on a base line. A dark-to-light gradient wedge image sits between the icons and the panel, navy #262F47 at its upper-left edge fading to white/light grey toward the panel, tapering in width from top to bottom and overlapping the panel's left edge. No photographs. The Sunoco LP wordmark logo is bottom-left in navy #262F47 bold italic.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the whole slide. White background. Panel is flat #F2F2F2 with square corners and no shadow, with the gradient wedge as its only shading. Title is bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) in red-orange #FA4931, centred, about 4.9% of slide height. Section headings are bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) in navy #262F47 at about 3.9% of slide height, centred, each underlined by a navy #262F47 horizontal rule about 0.5% of slide height thick. Body text is regular sans-serif (Arial) in navy #262F47 at about 2.3% of slide height with generous line spacing, bullets as solid dots. Footnote is italic Arial in grey #7F7F7F at about 1.1%. Page number is regular Arial navy #262F47 at about 1.6%. Footer rule is a red-orange #FA4931 line about 0.3% thick.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Combines a bar chart of segment AEBITDA with a line chart of WTI crude prices over the same timeline, showing performance against commodity cycles on one slide.
- Review: Most content pages state their point in the title, and the charts are readable, for example the dual-axis chart on slide 14 with its COVID callout and the DCF growth chart on slide 10.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent navy and orange system, the claim-style titles, the cover photo collage (slide 1), and the clear annotated charts on slides 10, 11 and 14.

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a full-width title block at the top, centred, two lines. Below it a combined bar-and-line chart spans the middle, with a data table beneath it, a legend under the chart, and a footer band at the bottom holding the SUNOCO LP logo at the left, a thin red rule, footnotes in two columns and the page number at the far right. Left margin about 4 percent, right margin about 4 percent; the title is centred on the slide.

**Visuals**

One combination chart. Type: vertical bar chart (Fuel Volume, navy #262F47) with a line overlay (Fuel Distribution AEBITDA, red #FA4931). Left y-axis titled 'Segment AEBITDA ($MM)' with labelled ticks $200, $450, $700, $950 (linear, $200 to $950). Right y-axis titled 'Billion Gallons' with ticks 2, 4, 6, 8, 10. X-axis categories 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, TTM 2Q25. Values are not printed; estimated Fuel Volume bars in billion gallons: about 8.2 (2019), about 7.1 (2020), about 7.5 (2021), about 7.7 (2022), about 8.3 (2023), about 8.6 (2024), about 9.0 (TTM 2Q25). Estimated Fuel Distribution AEBITDA line in $MM: about 560 (2019), about 575 (2020), about 625 (2021), about 740 (2022), about 800 (2023), about 895 (2024), about 910 (TTM 2Q25). Legend below the chart: red line symbol labelled 'Fuel Distribution AEBITDA(2)' and navy square labelled 'Fuel Volume(2)'. A grey vertical gradient band (about #BFBFBF at top fading to white at bottom) spans 2021 to 2024 behind the bars, labelled 'High Inflation and Interest Rates' at its top. A dashed-outline box at upper left, over the 2019 and 2020 area, contains the COVID note. Table below the chart: 2 rows by 7 columns with a header column. Row 1 'Year-over-Year Gasoline Demand (3)': -13.5%, +9.5%, -0.1%, +1.5%, +0.2%, -1.8% (columns 2019 to TTM 2Q25 order as the chart categories). Row 2 'Average WTI ($/bbl)': $39.20, $68.00, $94.80, $77.60, $76.60, $67.32. Table has black 1 pt grid lines and a black outer border. No photographs.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) in red-orange #FA4931, about 4.9 percent of slide height per line, two centred lines. Body and axis text in regular Arial-style sans-serif, navy #262F47, about 1.6 percent of slide height. Footnotes in italic sans-serif, grey #7F7F7F, about 1.1 percent. Accent colours: navy #262F47 for bars and table text, red #FA4931 for the line, title and footer rule. Table text bold in sans-serif. Line and shape styles: chart lines about 4 pt thick in red; axes thin grey lines; footer rule red about 4 pt; dashed box with thin navy dashed outline (about 1 pt) and no fill; grey gradient band with no outline; no corner radii, shadows or 3D effects.

### Slide 15

**Text**

SUN Well Positioned Within Industry Dynamics / Industry Facing Escalating / Costs(1) and Flat/Lower / Fuel Demand(2) / Wages: +49% / Credit Card Fees: +42% / Fuel Demand: -6% / Indexed to FY19 / 2019 1H25 / Wages / Credit Card / Fees / Fuel / Demand / Higher fuel margin required to offset / higher operating costs and/or / flat/decreasing store gross profit / Cost Structure and Scale / Determines Impact of / Higher Breakeven Margin / Illustrative Retail Margin Example / Marginal Breakeven Cost / Inflation increases / marginal breakeven cost / 600 / 500 / 400 / 300 / 200 / 100 / 0 / More Efficient / Less Efficient / Operator Cost Structure / SUN is a low-cost operator with / industry leading scale of over 15 / billion gallons per year / SUN’s History of Managing / Expenses While Growing / AEBITDA / $MM / 2019 2020 2021 2022 2023 2024 TTM / 2Q25 / Fuel Distribution Expenses(3) / Fuel Distribution operating expenses / have decreased on average 0.6% / annually (2019 – TTM 2Q25 CAGR) / Fuel Distribution AEBITDA(4) has / grown on average 7.6% annually / (2019 – TTM 2Q25 CAGR) / SUNOCO LP / 15 / (1) National Association of Convenience Store, C-Store Exchange database / (2) EIA, U.S. Product Supplied of Finished Motor Gasoline / (3) Prior to current segment reporting in 2023, expenses were not allocated proportionally by segment / (4) AEBITDA is a non-GAAP number. For a definition, please see slide 2

**Layout**

Three columns of equal width separated by two thin vertical navy divider lines at about x 31 and x 67, each column headed by a navy rectangular header panel with white bold centred text; the slide title is centred across the top in red bold, with empty space between the title and the column headers. A footer band holds the SUNOCO LP logo at bottom left, a thin red rule running from about x 17 to x 92 at y 96, the page number 15 at bottom right and two columns of grey footnotes beneath the rule; margins are about 1.3% left and right.

**Visuals**

Left: clustered column chart titled by the header 'Industry Facing Escalating Costs(1) and Flat/Lower Fuel Demand(2)', indexed to FY19 on the y-axis (no numeric tick labels printed; values about 100 for every 2019 bar, about 149 for 1H25 Wages, about 142 for 1H25 Credit Card Fees, about 94 for 1H25 Fuel Demand). Categories 2019 and 1H25. Series: Wages navy #262F47, Credit Card Fees red #FA4931, Fuel Demand light grey #B9BCC4; legend below the chart with square markers. A dashed red-outlined callout box at top left of the chart reads 'Wages: +49% / Credit Card Fees: +42% / Fuel Demand: -6%'. Middle: schematic diagram titled 'Illustrative Retail Margin Example' in red. A vertical navy y-axis labelled 'Marginal Breakeven Cost' and a horizontal x-axis labelled 'Operator Cost Structure' with a navy double-headed arrow bar reading 'More Efficient' (left) and 'Less Efficient' (right). Two navy double-line S-shaped curves rise from a SUNOCO logo at the origin, the upper curve ending in an arrowhead at about x 63 y 37 and the lower, flatter curve ending in an arrowhead at about x 63 y 45. A solid navy upward block arrow at about x 50.5 y 40 sits between the curves, with a dashed red-outlined callout to its right reading 'Inflation increases marginal breakeven cost'. The SUNOCO logo is a yellow hexagonal badge with a navy outlined SUNOCO wordmark and red arrow flourishes at its corners. Right: navy column chart titled 'Fuel Distribution Expenses(3)' with y-axis '$MM' ranging 0 to 600 in steps of 100 (gridlines absent), categories 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, TTM 2Q25. No values are printed; estimated values: 2019 about 433, 2020 about 417, 2021 about 388, 2022 about 433, 2023 about 481 (tallest), 2024 about 427, TTM 2Q25 about 416. All bars navy, no legend, no highlighted points. Below the charts are three red bullet points as text, and the footer SUNOCO LP wordmark logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF). Title in heavy sans-serif bold (Arial Bold or Helvetica Bold) red #FA4931, about 4.9% of slide height, centred. Column headers in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold), white #FFFFFF on navy panels #262F47, about 2.3% of slide height, centred. Body and chart text in regular sans-serif (Arial), navy #262F47, about 1.5% to 2.0% of slide height; axis titles and category labels about 1.5% to 1.6%. Bullets are red #FA4931 solid round dots. Accent colours: red #FA4931 for the title, bullets, bar series and callouts; pure red #FF0000 for the 'Illustrative Retail Margin Example' heading. Navy #262F47 for headers, bars, divider lines and the diagram. Grey #B9BCC4 for the fuel demand series, #7F7F7F for footnotes. Rectangular shapes with square corners, no shadows, no gradients. Dashed red 0.75 pt outlines on the two callout boxes. Divider lines are solid navy about 0.3 pt wide. Footer red rule about 0.4% of slide height thick.

### Slide 16

**Text**

Geographic Diversity Creates Stability / with Growth Opportunities
United States
Greater Caribbean
Canada
Largest independent fuel / distributor in the U.S.
Proven income stability with / consistent growth
Opportunity for channel / optimization on newly / acquired Parkland assets
Provides fuel for one in five / fuel locations in Canada
History of higher sustained / margins compared to U.S.
Opportunity for channel / optimization for further / income stability
Largest fuel distributor in the / Greater Caribbean
Composed of several unique / markets with attractive / margins and pockets of high / demand growth(1)
(1) Source: IMF, World Economic Outlook Database, April 2025; GDP growth from 2025 – 2030 includes Grenada (27%), Dominican Republic (40%), Guyana (59%) and Suriname (180%)
16

**Layout**

Three equal dark rounded columns sit side by side in a centred row spanning about 10.8% to 89.2% of the slide width, from about 18% to 90% of slide height, with a gap of about 2.8% between columns. The title is centred across the top in two lines at about 3% to 14% of height. Each column stacks a centred heading with a white underline, a map, and three centred body statements. A thin orange-red rule runs left to right across the footer at about 95.4% of height, with the Sunoco LP logo at its left and the page number at its right, and the source footnote is centred beneath it at about 97% of height.

**Visuals**

Three map illustrations of fuel distribution sites, all on white or pale grey base maps. Left, United States: a 401 by 310 pixel map of the contiguous US in dark navy #262F47 states with many small bright blue #3A8DFF dots for US Fuel Sites, grey #E0E0E0 Canada and Mexico and grey ocean, with a small inset box at lower left labelled Hawaii showing the islands in navy with blue dots; a legend at lower right reads US Fuel Sites (blue dot) and US Fuel Distribution (navy square); the map is boxed with square corners and no overlay. Centre, Canada: a map of Canada in navy #262F47 with blue dots concentrated along the southern border and in the east, cropped at the top and sides, on white with grey US and Greenland areas, shown as a single image split into three horizontal slices; no legend or labels. Right, Greater Caribbean: a 401 by 310 pixel map of the Caribbean and Central America on white with grey land; navy #262F47 islands (Dominican Republic, Puerto Rico, Cuba, Jamaica area, Guyana, Suriname, French Guiana, Honduras and Belize area) with blue #3A8DFF dots marking fuel sites on smaller islands; an inset box at upper right labelled Bermuda showing navy island with blue dots; a legend at right reads Caribbean Fuel Sites (blue dot) and Caribbean Fuel Distribution (navy square). The Sunoco LP logo at lower left is a bold italic wordmark in navy #262F47 with the letters SUNOCO in heavy caps and LP smaller.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the whole slide. Clean white corporate slide with a bold orange-red headline and white text on dark navy columns. Title text and rule are orange-red #FA4931, column panels and page number are dark navy #262F47, body and headings are white #FFFFFF, footnote is mid grey #7F7F7F, map oceans are white and land is light grey #E0E0E0 with navy #262F47 for the regions of interest and bright blue #3A8DFF dots. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-like) sans-serif: title Arial Bold about 4.9% of slide height (cap height about 3.4%), column headings Arial Bold about 3.3%, body Arial Regular about 2.3%, footnote Arial Italic about 1.1%, page number Arial Regular about 1.6%. Column panels have large rounded corners with radius about 16% of panel width on top and bottom, with no shadow and no border. White underlines under each heading are about 0.2% of slide height thick.

### Slide 17

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Shrink or move the legal text on slide 2 to an appendix, enlarge or cut the footnotes, and strengthen contrast on the grey overview panels and faded icons on slides 13, 17, 21 and 25.

**Text**

Diverse and Stable Business Segments
Terminals Overview
• Multi-geography, multi-commodity independent terminal system:
• 53 crude and refined product terminals(1) in continental United States
• 18 crude and refined product terminals in Europe(2)
• 13 refined product terminals in the Greater Caribbean
• Nine terminals in Canada
• Six refined product terminals in Hawaii
• Leading transmix processor in the U.S. with four facilities
Key SUN Investment Highlights
• Terminals will remain high-value, critical infrastructure for decades
• Vertical integration maximizes the value of terminals
• SUN strategy targets critical energy infrastructure in key European markets
17
(1) Excludes terminals connected to the Pipeline Systems segment
(2) Includes pending acquisition of TanQuid (expected close in the fourth quarter of 2025)

**Layout**

Single-column composition on a white slide: a bold red title is centred across the top, and a light grey content panel sits to the right of a column of four grey line icons on the left. The panel holds two centred section headings, each with a full-width dark rule beneath it, and bulleted text left-aligned inside the panel. A thin red rule runs along the bottom, with the Sunoco LP logo at the bottom-left, the slide number at the bottom-right, and two footnotes beneath the rule.

**Visuals**

Four grey monoline icons in a vertical column at the left edge, all with white fill and grey outline (#8C8C8C), each about 10 to 12% of slide width: (1) a tank truck with a cylindrical tank and cab, top; (2) two upright cylindrical storage tanks of different diameters, second; (3) a pipeline section with a gate valve on top and two elbow pipe fittings, third; (4) a refinery or petrochemical plant with distillation columns and vessels, bottom. A horizontal gradient wedge (dark navy #262F47 at the left fading to white) runs from the icon column to the grey panel, narrow at the icon side and spanning the full panel height at the panel edge; it is a tapered shape, not a photograph. The Sunoco LP wordmark logo in dark navy italic bold sits bottom-left. No chart or table.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. The title is bold red-orange sans-serif (Arial Bold style) #FA4931, centred. Section headings are bold dark navy sans-serif (Arial Bold style) #262F47, centred. Body text is regular Arial-style sans-serif #262F47. Title text is about 4.9% of slide height; section headings about 3.9%; body about 2.3%; footnotes about 1.1% in grey #7F7F7F italic. Dark navy rules #262F47 about 0.4% of height thick under each heading. Red #FA4931 rule about 0.4% thick along the bottom. Grey panel #F2F2F2 with square corners and no shadow. Icons are grey outline line art.

**Text**

Terminals Will Remain High-Value, / Critical Infrastructure for Decades
Terminal Infrastructure / Remains Essential

• As total storage capacity declines, remaining / terminals experience stronger demand

• The importance of waterborne terminals will / increase in major trading hubs (e.g., New York / Harbor, Amsterdam/Rotterdam/Antwerp, and U.S. / West Coast) as refinery closures continue

**Layout**

Two-column layout below a centred two-line title: a bulleted text column on the left (about 5 to 50% of width) and a bar chart column on the right (about 51 to 96% of width), each with a bold centred heading and a navy rule beneath it. The Sunoco LP logo sits bottom-left, a thin orange rule runs along the footer to the page number 18 at bottom-right, and the source footnote sits beneath the rule, left-aligned at about 16% of width.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart, untitled apart from the right-column heading 'Total U.S. Storage Capacity Has Plateaued(1)'. Y-axis: 'Millions of Barrels', linear, range 500 to 1,500, ticks at 500, 750, 1,000, 1,250, 1,500. X-axis: years 2014 to 2024, one bar per year (11 bars). One series, solid navy #262F47 bars of equal width with small gaps, no data labels printed, no legend, no gridlines. Bar values are not printed; estimates: 2014 about 1,080; 2015 about 1,130; 2016 about 1,175; 2017 about 1,220; 2018 about 1,250; 2019 about 1,265; 2020 about 1,325; 2021 about 1,390 (highest); 2022 about 1,355; 2023 about 1,345; 2024 about 1,340. No points are highlighted in a different colour. The Sunoco LP logo (navy wordmark with 'LP', italic bold sans-serif) is a raster image at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the whole slide. Clean white background with no ornament. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) in red-orange #FA4931, centred, two lines, cap height about 4.9% of slide height. Column headings in bold Arial, navy #262F47, about 2.9% of height, centred over each column. Body bullets in regular Arial, navy #262F47, about 2.3% of height, with orange #FA4931 bullet dots. Navy #262F47 rules about 0.3% of height thick under each heading. Chart bars navy #262F47, axis labels regular Arial about 1.6% of height, navy #262F47. Footer rule orange #FA4931, about 0.4% of height thick. Source line italic Arial in grey #7F7F7F, about 1.1% of height. Page number regular Arial navy #262F47. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners.

### Slide 20

**Text**

SUN Strategy Targets Critical Energy
Infrastructure in Key European Markets
Attractive Industry Fundamentals
• Refinery closures drive increased demand for
terminals and storage infrastructure
• Stricter regulations create barriers to building new
capacity
• Renewable fuels require more extensive storage
solutions than traditional oil and refined products
• Geopolitical developments alter traditional supply
sources, increasing the need for flexibility
Highlights from Recent Investments
• Terminals support European government reserve
programs, enabling long-term lease stability
• Terminals in Ireland and Amsterdam are fully leased
• German terminals’ river access will continue to
support long-term petroleum logistics as additional
refineries close
20
Note: Includes pending acquisition of TanQuid (expected close in the fourth quarter of 2025)

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: a title block spanning the top centre, a map occupying the left column (about x 1 to 49, y 23 to 74), and a right column (about x 51 to 96) holding two headed bullet sections stacked from top to bottom. Headings are centred over their navy rules in the right column. A logo sits bottom-left, an orange rule runs across the bottom with the page number at bottom-right, and a grey note sits under the rule at bottom-left.

**Visuals**

A map of western and central Europe in flat grey tones, cropped at the left, top and bottom edges and not framed. Ireland (labelled IE), the Netherlands (labelled NL), Germany (labelled DE) and Poland (labelled PL) are darker grey (about #7F7F7F); other countries are light grey (about #E3E3E3) with thin white borders. Red circular markers with white outlines (about #E8231A) sit at about 13 locations: one in western Ireland, one in the Netherlands near Amsterdam, about six in western and southern Germany, one in northern-central Germany and one in eastern Germany, and one in central Poland. The map is built from three stacked image tiles spanning the full map width. No chart, table or photograph is present. The SUNOCO LP logo is shown at bottom-left as a dark navy wordmark in bold italic capitals with a small 'LP' suffix.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with no decorative shapes beyond thin rules. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) in orange-red #FA4931, centred, about 4.9% of slide height per line. Section headings in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) in navy #262F47, about 2.9% of slide height, centred. Body text in regular sans-serif (Arial style) in navy #262F47, about 2.3% of slide height, with orange-red #FA4931 bullet dots. Navy #262F47 rules of about 0.4% height sit under each heading. A thin orange-red #FA4931 rule runs along the footer. Note text is italic grey #7F7F7F at about 1.1% of slide height. Page number in regular navy #262F47. No gradients, shadows or rounded corners.

Four line icons in a vertical column at the left margin. Icon 1 (top): a grey outline tank truck, a cylindrical tanker on a chassis with three wheels, facing right. Icon 3: navy outline of a pipe assembly with a gate valve and handwheel on a vertical pipe, with two downward-facing flanged pipe ends. Icon 4 (bottom): grey outline of a refinery or processing plant with towers, vessels and pipes. A horizontal gradient wedge, dark navy #262F47 at the left tip fading to light grey #F2F2F2 at the right edge, tapers from the left of the grey panel toward the left margin, with its point at mid-height around 57%. No photographs or charts. Red footer rule and navy and red logo are graphic elements.

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title in bold red-orange sans-serif (Arial Bold style), colour #FA4931, large at about 4.9% of slide height. Section headings in bold navy sans-serif (Arial Bold style), colour #262F47, about 3.9% of slide height. Body bullets in regular navy sans-serif (Arial style), colour #262F47, about 2.3% of slide height. Page number in regular navy sans-serif, about 1.6%. Icons in grey (#A6A6A6 approx) except icon 3 which is navy #262F47. Gradient wedge runs dark navy to light grey horizontally.

### Slide 23

**Text**

Critical Energy Infrastructure Pipeline Systems / ND / MN / SD / IA / NE / KS / Mid-Continent Refined / Products System / Southwest Crude and / Refined Products System / Ammonia System / CO / OK / NM / TX / MO / IL / IN / IA / NE / AR / LA / • Integrated pipeline and terminal / network across six states supports / domestic agricultural and / transportation fuel demand / • Extensive refinery connectivity / throughout the region provides / optionality and reliability for shippers / • Growing suite of blending / opportunities across all systems allow / for incremental value to both / customers and SUN / • Multiple SUN terminals and third- / party connection points in key / markets / • Comprehensive crude and refined / products pipeline systems ensure / ratable, safe, and efficient supply and / delivery throughout the region which / are critical for refinery operation and / consumer demand / • SUN is well-positioned to support / growing markets across the region, / both domestic and export / • Only interstate ammonia pipeline in / the U.S., spanning 2,000 miles from / the Gulf Coast to the Midwest / • Agricultural demand expected to / remain robust and structurally / exclusive / • SUN is uniquely positioned to / transport low carbon and / decarbonized ammonia as the market / continues to develop / SUNOCO LP / 23

**Layout**

Three equal columns below a full-width title, each column holding a map on top, a bold section heading with a navy rule beneath it, and a bulleted list below. The title is centred at the top, starting about 4% from the top edge and spanning most of the width. Column left edges are about 3.4%, 36.6% and 69.2% of the width, with side margins of about 3.5%. Headings are centred over their columns. A thin orange-red rule runs across the lower part of the slide at about 94.6% of the height, from the SUNOCO LP logo at the left to a slide number at the bottom right, which sits at about 93.6% of the width.

**Visuals**

Three line maps of US states, each drawn in dark grey outlines on a light grey (#F5F5F5) fill with state abbreviations in grey (#7F7F7F) and no photographs. (1) Mid-Continent Refined Products System map: states ND, MN, SD, IA, NE and KS. Yellow (#FFE600) pipeline lines with small circular station markers run north-south through ND, SD, NE and KS, with an east-west line across ND, a branch from ND southeast into MN, a line from NE/IA border area northeast into IA, and a line running south-southwest from the NE/IA border to KS. (2) Southwest Crude and Refined Products System map: states CO, NM, OK and TX. Yellow pipelines run from northern CO southeast through NM and OK toward the TX panhandle, with an east-west yellow line in NM, a yellow line from the TX panhandle southwest to the TX border, a yellow line across central TX to the east coast and a cluster of yellow lines and red terminal dots along the Gulf coast of TX. Red (#E0201B) pipeline segments run through the TX panhandle into OK and along the Gulf coast near Houston. Red square dots mark terminals. (3) Ammonia System map: states NE, IA, IL, IN, MO, AR and LA. A thin orange (#F59B1E) dotted pipeline runs from NE northeast into IA, then south through IA, MO, AR and LA to the Gulf coast, with a branch from the IA/IL area east-northeast to IN. The map is cropped to the states shown with no border around it.

**Design**

Background: Solid white (#FFFFFF) background across the whole slide.

### Slide 24

**Text**

Joint Venture with Energy Transfer is a
Highly Efficient Permian Platform
Permian Basin Crude Gathering System
• Combination of SUN’s and ET’s pipelines creates expansive system in the Midland and Delaware Basins
• Multiple connections to long-haul pipelines provides optionality with customers
• Lowest breakevens among major crude basins support continued production growth
• High-quality customer base provides ratable income streams
• Ten counties in the Permian Basin have accounted for 93% of U.S. oil production growth since 2020(1)
NM
TX
50
45
40
SUN’s Share of AEBITDA (2)($MM)
3Q24 4Q24 1Q25 2Q25
24
(1) Source: EIA Today in Energy, published 9/2/25
(2) Adjusted EBITDA is a non-GAAP measure. For a definition, please see slide 2

**Layout**

Centred title block across the top with a two-line headline, a centred bold subtitle and a full-width navy rule beneath it. Below, a left column of five red-bulleted statements runs down to the chart area at the lower left, a map sits at the right beside the bullets, and a footer band carries the Sunoco LP logo at the left, a red rule, and the slide number at the right with two footnotes beneath.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart, four bars in navy #262F47 with no value labels printed. Y-axis title 'SUN’s Share of AEBITDA (2)($MM)', scale ticks 40, 45 and 50 with no unit beyond $MM, axis baseline at 40 (bottom) and top of the axis slightly above 50. Categories on the x-axis: 3Q24, 4Q24, 1Q25, 2Q25. Estimated bar heights read from the axis: 3Q24 about 44.2, 4Q24 about 45.3, 1Q25 about 47.4, 2Q25 about 48.6 ($MM). No legend; no highlighted points; bars are equal width with even gaps, the axis lines are dark navy. Map: a schematic network map of pipelines in the Permian Basin, drawn as thin red lines (about #E0201B) with small red node dots on a light grey #F4F4F4 field, showing the NM and TX state areas separated by a thin grey #7F7F7F state border line, with grey state labels NM (upper left) and TX (lower right). The network is densest on the right side around central and eastern Texas and sparser to the west and south; a single long line runs east off the right edge. The map is boxed by a thin black #000000 frame, not cropped and not full-bleed. Logo: Sunoco LP wordmark in bold navy #262F47 italic capitals with a small trademark mark, at lower left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial/Helvetica Bold), orange-red #FA4931, large, the two lines centred, about 4.9% of slide height per line. Subtitle in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold), navy #262F47, centred, about 2.9% of slide height. Body bullets in regular sans-serif (Arial), navy #262F47, about 2.3% of slide height, with orange-red #FA4931 round bullet dots. Chart labels and axis text in regular Arial, navy #262F47, about 1.6% of slide height. Footnotes in italic Arial, grey #7F7F7F, about 1.1% of slide height. Page number in regular Arial navy #262F47. Navy rule under subtitle about 3 px thick in #262F47 spanning nearly full width; orange-red rule in the footer about 4 px thick in #FA4931 with square ends. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners on the chart bars.

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Shrink or move the legal text on slide 2 to an appendix, enlarge or cut the footnotes, and strengthen contrast on the grey overview panels and faded icons on slides 13, 17, 21 and 25.

**Text**

Diverse and Stable Business Segments / Integrated Refinery and Marketing System / • Burnaby refinery was acquired by Parkland in 2017 from Chevron with / advantaged fuel distribution network in British Columbia / • #1 market share position with Chevron branded stations / • Exclusive licensing rights for the Chevron fuel brand in British / Columbia / • The acquisition included three terminals in British Columbia / • Burnaby refinery has ~55,000 bpd operational capacity / • 90% of refinery output supplies SUN customers / • Price-advantaged crudes – Trans Mountain Pipeline delivers / discounted feedstock to the West Coast / Key SUN Investment Highlights / • Consistently delivers positive cash flow / • On a consolidated basis in 2024, refinery would have accounted for only / ~5% of total SUN AEBITDA(1) / 25 / (1) Adjusted EBITDA is a non-GAAP measure. For a definition, please see slide 2

**Layout**

Single-column content layout: the bold red title is centred across the top of the slide, and a light grey panel fills the right of the slide from about 23% to 87% of the width with two centred-left headed sections stacked inside it. A column of four grey line icons runs down the left margin beside a tapered gradient wedge that joins the panel's left edge; the SUNOCO LP logo sits bottom left above a thin red footer rule with the slide number at bottom right and the footnote beneath the rule.

**Visuals**

Four line icons in a left column, all drawn in thin outline style: (1) a grey tanker truck with a cylindrical tank body, ladder rungs and two wheels, at the top; (2) grey storage tanks, one large and one small cylinder with vertical banding, in the middle; (3) a grey pipeline valve with a T-shaped handwheel on a pipe run with elbows and two flanges, below that; (4) a navy refinery icon of distillation columns, towers and pipes, at the bottom beside the wedge. A tapered gradient wedge (about 9% of slide width, 83% of height) sits between the icons and the grey panel: it is narrow at the top, widens toward the bottom, and fades from white at top to dark navy #262F47 at bottom-left, overlapping the panel's left edge. Two solid navy horizontal rules, about 0.3% of slide height thick, sit under the two section headings and span the panel's text width. A thin red #FA4931 horizontal rule runs across the footer between the logo and the page number. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White slide background. Title in bold red-orange #FA4931, sans-serif (Arial Bold or Helvetica Bold) at about 4.9% of slide height, sitting in the top band. Section headings in bold navy #262F47 sans-serif (Arial Bold) at about 3.9% of slide height. Body bullets in regular navy #262F47 sans-serif (Arial or Helvetica regular) at about 2.3% of slide height with generous line spacing. Footnote in italic grey #7F7F7F sans-serif at about 1.1% of slide height. Grey panel #F2F2F2 with square corners, no border, no shadow. Navy rules solid and square-ended. Red footer rule solid. Icons in grey #A6A6A6 line style except the navy refinery icon.

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a centred red title across the top, a full-width navy banner beneath it, a column bar chart in the middle band, two bullets below the chart, and a footer rule with logo at bottom left, footnote under the rule and page number at bottom right. Margins are about 8.8% on the left and right for the banner, and the chart's plot area runs from about 13% to 87% of the width.

**Visuals**

Bar chart, vertical columns, titled by the navy banner above it. Y-axis label: Segment AEBITDA(1) ($MM), with superscript (1) referring to the footnote; scale $0 to $400 with gridline-free tick labels at $0, $100, $200, $300 and $400. X-axis categories: 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, 1H25. Values are not printed; estimated values: 2018 about $220MM; 2019 about $270MM; 2020 about $85MM; 2021 about $195MM; 2022 about $275MM; 2023 about $235MM; 2024 about $100MM; 1H25 about $115MM. All eight columns are solid navy #262F47 with equal width and gaps, sitting on a thin light grey baseline. No legend. Two highlighted annotations: a dashed red (#FA4931) rectangle reading Extended Shutdown From Covid with a dashed red down arrow pointing to the 2020 column top; and a dashed red rectangle reading Unplanned Turnaround in 1Q24 with a dashed red down arrow pointing to the 2024 column top. Annotation boxes have white fill and sharp corners. Logo: SUNOCO LP wordmark in bold navy italic sans-serif capitals with a small registered mark, at bottom left, not cropped. Red horizontal rule runs between the logo and the page number.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a navy and red corporate palette. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) red #FA4931, about 4.9% of slide height, centred. Banner text in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) white #FFFFFF, about 2.9% of slide height, on a navy #262F47 rectangle with square corners and no shadow. Body bullets in regular sans-serif (Arial style) navy #262F47, about 2.3% of slide height, with red #FA4931 bullet dots. Chart labels in regular sans-serif (Arial style) navy #262F47, about 1.6% of slide height. Footnote in italic sans-serif grey #7F7F7F, about 1.1% of slide height. Page number regular navy #262F47. Lines: thick red #FA4931 footer rule about 0.5% of slide height; annotation boxes have dashed red outlines; no gradients or shadows.

### Slide 28

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a vertical timeline of acquisitions by year with labeled transaction boxes, presenting 35 deals in a compact visual history.
- What would change the score: I would trim the bullet-heavy pages to one message each, add a closing summary slide, and fix slide 28, where the timeline boxes run to the slide edge and wrap awkwardly.

**Text**

History of Buying at the “Right” Price & Integrating…
✓ AMID / Terminals / ✓ Superior Plus / Terminals / ✓ Six Fuel / Distribution / Bolt-Ons
✓ IPT Terminal
✓ Peerless Oil / and Gas / ✓ Gladieux / Transmix / ✓ Two Fuel / Distribution / Bolt-Ons
✓ NuStar Energy / ✓ Zenith Europe / Terminals / ✓ Pike Fuels / Terminal / ✓ One Fuel / Distribution / Bolt-On
2018 2019 2020 2021 2022 2023 2024 2025
✓ One Fuel / Distribution / Bolt-On
✓ NuStar East / Coast / Terminals / ✓ One Fuel / Distribution / Bolt-On
✓ Zenith North / America / Terminals / ✓ Cato Oil / Terminal / ✓ One Fuel / Distribution / Bolt-On
✓ Parkland / Corporation / ✓ TanQuid / ✓ 10 Fuel / Distribution / Bolt-Ons
35 Midstream and Fuel Distribution Transactions Since 2018
SUNOCO LP
28
Note: Includes pending acquisition of TanQuid (expected close in the fourth quarter of 2025)

**Layout**

Eight-column timeline with four rounded cards above and four below a central horizontal chevron bar, one card per year from 2018 to 2025 in left-to-right order, with alternating top and bottom placement. The title sits top-left spanning the width at about 3% from the top, left-aligned at about 2% margin. The subtitle is centred near the bottom, the logo is bottom-left, an orange rule runs along the footer with the page number at bottom-right, and the footnote sits bottom-left under the logo.

**Visuals**

Timeline diagram. A single horizontal bar of dark navy #262F47 spanning about 6% to 95% of slide width at about 44% to 52% of slide height, made of eight contiguous right-pointing chevron segments, one per year, each about 15.5% wide, with white year labels 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025 set in the segments. Eight rounded cards filled #CBD1E3 with a thick navy #262F47 outline are connected to the bar by thin navy vertical lines: 2018 card above (AMID Terminals, Superior Plus Terminals, Six Fuel Distribution Bolt-Ons), 2019 card below (One Fuel Distribution Bolt-On), 2020 card above (IPT Terminal), 2021 card below (NuStar East Coast Terminals, One Fuel Distribution Bolt-On), 2022 card above (Peerless Oil and Gas, Gladieux Transmix, Two Fuel Distribution Bolt-Ons), 2023 card below (Zenith North America Terminals, Cato Oil Terminal, One Fuel Distribution Bolt-On), 2024 card above (NuStar Energy, Zenith Europe Terminals, Pike Fuels Terminal, One Fuel Distribution Bolt-On), 2025 card below (Parkland Corporation, TanQuid, 10 Fuel Distribution Bolt-Ons). Each list item is prefixed by a navy check mark. Logo: SUNOCO LP wordmark in bold italic navy #262F47 at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style), orange-red #FA4931, about 4.9% of slide height, not capitalised beyond normal sentence case. Card and body text in regular sans-serif (Arial style), navy #262F47, about 2.3% of slide height. Year labels white regular sans-serif about 3.3% of slide height. Subtitle bold sans-serif #FA4931 about 3.3% of slide height. Cards have square-ish rounded corners of about 3.5% of slide width radius and a navy outline about 0.6% of slide height thick, no shadows, no gradients. Connector lines navy about 0.4% of slide height thick. Footer rule orange #FA4931 about 0.4% thick. Footnote italic grey #7F7F7F about 1.1% of slide height.

**Layout**

Single full-width chart slide on a white field. The red title is centred across the top with no subtitle band beneath it; a full-width navy banner sits below it, with the bar chart filling the area beneath the banner from the left margin to the right margin. The sunoco logo sits bottom-left, a thin red rule runs across the lower part of the slide to the right of the logo, the page number 29 sits bottom-right, and a small italic footnote sits under the chart on the left.

**Visuals**

Chart: combined bar and line chart. Title: none on the chart itself; the navy banner above it reads SUN is the Only AMZI Constituent to Grow DCF(1) per Common Unit for the Last Eight Consecutive Years (2017-2024). Y-axis: Dollars per common unit, $2.50 to $6.00, gridlines absent, tick labels every $0.50 at $2.50, $3.00, $3.50, $4.00, $4.50, $5.00, $5.50, $6.00. X-axis: categories 2017, 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, TTM 2Q25, the axis baseline is a thin light grey line at $2.50. Series 1, DCF(1) per Common Unit, navy #262F47 bars, values about: 2017 $3.49, 2018 $3.88, 2019 $3.89, 2020 $4.27, 2021 $4.48, 2022 $5.10, 2023 $5.17, 2024 $5.60, TTM 2Q25 $5.67 (values estimated from bar heights, no printed values). Series 2, Distribution per Common Unit, green #22B14C line of about 3 pt with flat steps, values about: 2017 $3.36, 2018 $3.36, 2019 $3.36, 2020 $3.36, 2021 $3.36, 2022 $3.36, 2023 $3.50, 2024 $3.68, TTM 2Q25 $3.84 (estimated, no printed values). Legend: below the plot, centred, navy square marker for DCF(1) per Common Unit and green line marker for Distribution per Common Unit. Highlighted point: a red #FA4931 block arrow with a thin navy outline runs diagonally from lower-left (above the 2017 bar, about $4.05) to upper-right (above the 2024 bar, about $5.85), carrying the white label ~7% CAGR (2017 – TTM 2Q25) along its length. Two small white rectangles sit over the chart near the arrow's tail and head. The bars and line are drawn over the plain white background with no shadows.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the full slide. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title in red #FA4931, bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style), approximately 4.9% of slide height, centred. Banner text in white #FFFFFF, bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style), approximately 2.9% of slide height, centred over two lines. Banner fill navy #262F47, square corners, no shadow. Axis and legend text navy #262F47, regular sans-serif (Arial style), approximately 1.6% of slide height. Chart bars navy #262F47, square corners, no outline. Distribution line green, about #22B14C. Accent rule and arrow red #FA4931. Footnote text grey #7F7F7F, italic sans-serif, approximately 1.1% of slide height. Page number navy #262F47, regular sans-serif, approximately 1.6% of slide height.

### Slide 30

**Text**

Future Growth Opportunities Across / Various Geographies and Segments / 1 / United States / • Midstream Expansion / • Fuel Distribution / Bolt-Ons / 2 / Canada / • Midstream Expansion / • Fuel Distribution / Bolt-Ons / 3 / Greater Caribbean / • Midstream Expansion / • Fuel Distribution / Bolt-Ons / 4 / Europe / • Midstream Expansion / Europe / Hawaii / Fuel Distribution Operations / Contracted Fuel Locations / Transmix/Race Fuels / Terminals / Burnaby Refinery / Pipelines / 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 / (1) Includes pending acquisition of TanQuid (expected close in the fourth quarter of 2025) / 30

**Layout**

Two-column layout: a large map of North America fills the left of the slide (about x 2 to 71, y 17 to 90) with a numbered-list column on the right (about x 77 to 97) holding four numbered region entries stacked vertically. The title is centred at the top in two lines (y 3 to 14), and the slide is otherwise flush to the left margin for the map. The SUNOCO LP logo sits bottom-left at y 94, a thin orange rule runs horizontally at y 95 from the logo to the page number, the footnote sits below at y 96.6, and the page number 30 is bottom-right at x 93.6.

**Visuals**

Map of North America (Mercator style, flat fills) with the United States, Canada, Mexico and the Caribbean drawn in dark navy #29334B where Fuel Distribution Operations exist, and lighter grey #E0E0E0 for other land and the ocean white #FFFFFF. Blue #3D8EF0 dots mark Contracted Fuel Locations, densest across the eastern United States and Ontario/Quebec. Red #E8191F dots mark Terminals along the coasts, the Great Lakes and Texas. Small green #5BD62E dots mark Transmix/Race Fuels, one purple #A020A0 triangle marks the Burnaby Refinery near Vancouver, and yellow #F2E22A lines mark Pipelines running mainly through the central United States and Texas. Four orange numbered badges overlay the map: 1 over the central United States, 2 over Alberta/Saskatchewan, 3 over the Caribbean Sea, 4 over the Europe inset. An inset titled Europe (rounded rectangle with thin grey #A6A6A6 border, grey land #9E9E9E with a lighter surround, red terminal dots clustered over Germany, Poland and Benelux, and one dot in Ireland) sits in the upper right of the map. An inset titled Hawaii (rounded rectangle, thin grey border, navy islands with blue dots and red terminal dots) sits bottom-left. A legend with navy, blue, green, red, purple triangle and yellow line swatches sits right of the map at about x 56 to 70, y 38 to 52. The map is flat, unshaded and uncropped apart from the Europe and Hawaii insets.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold or Helvetica Bold), orange-red #FA4931, centred, two lines, cap height large (font size about 4.9 percent of slide height). Region headings in bold sans-serif #29334B, about 2.9 percent of slide height. Bullet text in regular sans-serif #262F47, about 2.3 percent of slide height. Numbered badges are filled circles #FA4931 with white bold numerals and a thin navy #29334B outline on the right column. Bullets are small orange #FA4931 dots. Footnote is italic sans-serif #7F7F7F at about 1.1 percent of slide height. Page number is regular sans-serif #262F47. Rule is solid orange #FA4931, about 0.4 percent of slide height thick. No shadows or gradients.
