**Layout**

Two-column layout below a centred two-line red title spanning the top; the left column holds a navy header box over a numbered S-curve diagram, the right column holds a navy header box over a pie chart. A full-width bold red takeaway sentence sits centred near the bottom, above a footer with the SUNOCO LP logo at the left, a thin red rule running to a page number at the right, and a grey source footnote beneath the rule. Left and right content edges sit at about 5.5% and 97% of width; the title is centred and the header boxes are left-aligned to their columns.

**Visuals**

Chart 1, a schematic line diagram with no printed values: a navy S-shaped curve with double stroke rising from lower left to an arrowhead at upper right, on a vertical navy axis labelled Marginal Breakeven Cost (rotated) and a horizontal double-headed navy arrow bar labelled More Efficient at the left end and Less Efficient at the right end, with the axis caption Operator Cost Structure beneath it. Three red numbered circles (1, 2, 3) sit on or beside the curve in ascending order from upper right to lower left, with red arrows pointing from label 1 down toward label 2 and from label 2 down toward label 3. A SUNOCO logo (yellow diamond with navy lettering and red arrows) sits on the curve near label 3. Chart 2, a pie chart with no axes and no legend: title Highly Fragmented Sector – 61% of Sites are Single Store Operators with superscript (1); slices start at 12 o'clock and run clockwise: about 22% (500+ Site Operator) in light grey, about 3% (201-500 Site Operator) grey, about 5% (51-200 Site Operator) grey, about 6% (11-50 Site Operator) grey, about 3% (2-10 Site Operator) grey, and about 61% (Single Site Operator) in red #FA4931 occupying the left and lower-left of the circle; the red slice is the highlighted point. Slices are separated by thin white radial lines and labels sit outside each slice in navy. Logos: SUNOCO logo on curve; SUNOCO LP logo in footer, navy with a small LP mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title text in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) red #FA4931, large; section headers white bold sans-serif on navy #262F47 rectangles with square corners and no shadow; body labels bold sans-serif navy #262F47; takeaway sentence bold sans-serif pure red #FF0000; accents red #FA4931 (numbered circles, arrows, pie slice, footer rule); grey slices #B9BCC4; footnote italic grey #7F7F7F. Title height about 4.9% of slide height per line; body labels about 2.0%; header text about 2.6%; takeaway about 3.3%; footnote about 1.1%. Shapes are flat with no gradients or shadows; the curve has a double navy stroke about 0.4% of slide height wide; the pie has white separator lines about 0.2% wide.

### Slide 32

**Text**

Advantaged Supply Cost from Leading Scale / in the Atlantic Basin / Scale / Largest independently(1) contracted fuel / demand in the Atlantic Basin with / >7 billion gallons / + / Footprint / Leading network of terminal positions from / Canada to South America / + / Expertise / Waterborne access maximizes / sourcing options / = / Leading Supply Cost Advantage / Europe / North Africa / Gulf Coast / South America / Pipelines / Terminal(2) / 32 / (1) Defined as: ability to supply from any supplier in any geography / (2) Includes 3rd party terminals where SUN has throughput capacity

**Layout**

Two-column slide. A two-line bold orange title is centred across the top (lines at about y 3% and 9%). A map panel occupies the left column from x 5.2% to 48.4% and y 14.5% to 93.1%. A right column from about x 56% to 92% holds three stacked blocks (Scale, Footprint, Expertise), each a centred heading over a dark rule, joined by orange plus signs, with an equals sign and a closing bold line 'Leading Supply Cost Advantage' at about y 84%. A thin orange footer rule runs at about y 94.6% from x 16.6% to 92.4%, with the SUNOCO LP logo at bottom left and page number 32 at bottom right; footnotes sit at the bottom left above the page edge.

**Visuals**

Map of the eastern United States, Canada east, the Atlantic, the Caribbean and northern South America, drawn with dark navy #262F47 land, pale grey #E0E0E0 neighbouring land and pale light-blue #E6F3F8 water. Red terminal markers (#E8201E with white outline) are placed along the US Atlantic and Gulf coasts from Maine to Florida, around the Great Lakes and St Lawrence area, and on Caribbean islands and the Guyana/Suriname coast. Orange-red #FA4931 block arrows show flows: a Pipelines arrow runs diagonally from the Gulf area up-right across the Appalachian region; a curved arrow from Europe sweeps up to Canada and across to the US coast and the Caribbean; arrows from Gulf Coast curve east and north-east; arrows from North Africa and Europe sweep west across the Atlantic; a South America arrow curves up from the bottom through the Caribbean to the Gulf area. Legend box at bottom centre shows an orange dot labelled Terminal(2). Three plus signs and one equals sign are solid orange #FA4931 symbols on the right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Headline and map labels use bold sans-serif, similar to Arial Bold, in orange #FA4931. Section headings and body text use regular sans-serif similar to Arial in dark navy #29334B; the closing line is bold navy. Title is about 4.9% of slide height; section headings about 2.9%; body about 2.3%; footnotes about 1.1%. Dark navy horizontal rules about 0.3% of height under each heading. Orange plus and equals symbols are flat with no shadows. Map labels are white on orange #FA4931 rectangles with no corner radius and no shadow. Map has no border.

### Slide 33

**Review notes**

- Review: The ending is weak, since slide 33 on midstream multiples is followed only by a logo page with no summary or call to action, and the deck mostly lists claims rather than building to a conclusion.

**Text**

Growing Midstream Portfolio
Small Midstream
Example: AMID (2018) – Initial high single-digit acquisition multiple was reduced to mid
single-digit multiple through increased throughput and improved utilization
Medium Midstream
Example: NuStar East Coast Terminals (2021) – Integrated with East Coast Fuel Distribution
business and reduced expenses resulting in mid single-digit synergized multiple
Large Midstream
Example: NuStar Energy (2024) – Reduced expenses by ~25% while maintaining volume and
reliability
Fuel Distribution and Midstream Combination
Example: Peerless (2022) – Doubled AEBITDA through self supply, expense management, and
other commercial synergies
Example: Parkland (2025) – Acquired 12 terminals in the Greater Caribbean, nine in Canada,
and eight terminals in U.S.
SUNOCO LP
33

**Layout**

A single left-aligned column of four stacked horizontal navy bars, each starting at a white circular badge on the left edge, with the bar headings in white inside the bars and the example bullets printed beneath each bar. The red title is centred at the top, a thin dark connector runs down the left edge through the badges, and the footer holds the SUNOCO LP logo at bottom left, a red rule across the bottom and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Four white circular badges with navy outlines, each holding a navy line-art icon of storage tanks or a tanker truck. Badge 1 (Small Midstream) shows two upright cylindrical tanks, a larger one and a smaller one beside it, with banding. Badge 2 (Medium Midstream) shows a larger tank and a smaller tank beside it. Badge 3 (Large Midstream) shows a large tank and a smaller tank beside it, both larger than in badge 2. Badge 4 (Fuel Distribution and Midstream Combination) shows a tanker truck on the left and a pair of tanks on the right. Connectors: navy line segments join the bottom of each badge to the top of the next, and short navy stubs enter the top-left and exit the bottom-left of the stack. No charts, photographs or photographs of any kind.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF). Title is bold red-orange (#FA4931) sans-serif (Arial Bold-like), about 4.9% of slide height. Bar headings are regular white (#FFFFFF) Arial-like sans-serif, about 3.9% of slide height. Body bullets are regular navy (#262F47) Arial-like sans-serif, about 2.3% of slide height, with red-orange (#FA4931) bullet dots. Navy bars (#262F47) are flat with square corners and no shadow. Badges are white circles with a navy (#262F47) outline of about 0.4% of slide width, no shadow. Connector lines are navy (#262F47). Red rule (#FA4931) in footer. No gradients.
